function timetableClassColorKey(room){
  const match=String(room||"").match(/(?:^|\b)([123])-?(\d{1,2})(?:\b|$)/);
  if(!match)return "";
  return `${match[1]}-${Number(match[2])}`;
}
function timetableClassColorClass(room){
  const key=timetableClassColorKey(room);
  if(!key)return "";
  let hash=0; for(const ch of key)hash=(hash*31+ch.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;
  return `class-color-${hash%9}`;
}

  return minute>=end?"night":"day";
}

let uepSchoolReadDiagnostics = [];
function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}

async function migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce(){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin" || state.settings?.programSheetMigrationV07662 || !window.schoolBoard?.saveProgramOperation) return;
  const overrides=state.programOverrides&&typeof state.programOverrides==="object"?state.programOverrides:{};
  const programs=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.programs)?readonlyCache.programs:[];
  let changed=false, failed=false;
  for(const [id,override] of Object.entries(overrides)){
    if(!override || !["date","time","place","teacher"].some(key=>String(override[key]??"").trim())) continue;
    const program=programs.find(item=>String(item.id||"")===String(id));
    if(!program?.source) continue;
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveProgramOperation({role:"admin",source:program.source,programId:program.programId||program.courseId||"",sessionId:program.sessionId||"",date:override.date??program.date??"",time:override.time??program.time??"",place:override.place??program.place??"",teacher:override.teacher??program.teacher??""});
      if(!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason||"시트 저장 실패");
      if(result?.programPatch)applyProgramWritePatch08172(program,result.programPatch);
      const kept={...override}; ["date","time","place","teacher"].forEach(key=>delete kept[key]);

function updateTopSyncStatus() {
  const button = $("#syncButton");
  if (!button) return;
  button.classList.remove("good", "warn", "bad");

function startReadonlyAutoRefresh(){
  if(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer);
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok) return;
  readonlyAutoRefreshTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    if(document.hidden) return;
    readonlyAutoRefreshCycle+=1;
    const fullSyncedAt=readonlyCache?.syncedAt?new Date(readonlyCache.syncedAt).getTime():0;
    const fullAge=fullSyncedAt?Date.now()-fullSyncedAt:Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const needsFull=!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS||readonlyAutoRefreshCycle%6===0;
    if(needsFull){
      const jitter=Math.floor(Math.random()*45000);
      setTimeout(()=>{if(!document.hidden)refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true});},jitter);
      return;
    }
    refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
  },READONLY_AUTO_REFRESH_MS);
}

function updateTopSyncStatus() {
  const button = $("#syncButton");
  if (!button) return;
  button.classList.remove("good", "warn", "bad");
  const label = button.querySelector("span");
  if (googleConnectionError) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "구글시트 오류";
    button.title = googleConnectionError;
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length && neisData?.ok && !neisData.offline) {
    button.classList.add("good");
    if (label) label.textContent = "동기화 정상";
    button.title = "UEP 구글시트와 NEIS 자료가 정상 연결되었습니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length) {
    button.classList.add("warn");
    if (label) label.textContent = neisData?.offline ? "NEIS 저장자료" : "NEIS 연결 대기";
    button.title = neisError || "UEP 구글시트는 연결되었고 NEIS 자료를 확인 중입니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (googleConnectionStatus?.ok) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "시트 권한 확인";
    button.title = googleConnectionError || "서비스 계정 이메일의 구글시트 공유 권한을 확인하세요.";
    return;
  }
  button.classList.add("warn");
  if (label) label.textContent = "인증 필요";
  button.title = "교사 이름·이메일 로그인과 기기승인 상태를 확인하세요.";
}

}
function currentSignalStudent(){
  // 신호등은 '현재 학생 상세 컨텍스트'가 확정된 경우에만 표시합니다.
  // 목록·통계·대시보드 등 단순 화면에서는 이전 selectedStudentId가 남아 있어도 표시하지 않습니다.
  const guidanceId=document.querySelector('#studentGuidanceLayer #studentGuidanceStudent')?.value||'';
  if(guidanceId)return studentForSignalId(guidanceId);

  const counselPanel=document.querySelector('.counsel-side-panel');
  if(counselPanel?.dataset?.studentSignalId)return studentForSignalId(counselPanel.dataset.studentSignalId);

  const drawer=document.getElementById('drawer');
  if(drawer&&!drawer.classList.contains('hidden')&&drawer.dataset.studentSignalId){
    return studentForSignalId(drawer.dataset.studentSignalId);
  }

  const page=String(state?.activePage||'');
  if(page==='students'){
    if(studentPageMode==='dashboard'&&selectedStudentId)return studentForSignalId(selectedStudentId);
    if(studentPageMode==='counsel'&&studentCo

===== readonly cache assignment #20 @ 58045 =====
ext?.studentId||'';
    return studentForSignalId(id);
  }
  if(page==='admissions'){
    const scope=document.getElementById('admissionScope')?.value||'individual';
    if(scope==='university')return null;
    const id=document.getElementById('admissionStudent')?.value||studentFocusContext?.studentId||'';
    return studentForSignalId(id);
  }
  if(page==='attendance'){
    if(attendanceMode==='statistics'&&attendanceStatStudentFilter&&attendanceStatStudentFilter!=='all'){
      const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
      return students.find(s=>attendanceStudentKey(s)===attendanceStatStudentFilter)||null;
    }
    if(studentFocusContext?.studentId)return studentForSignalId(studentFocusContext.studentId);
    return null;
  }
  if(page==='records'){
    if(recordQueryMode==='student'&&recordStudentId)return studentForSignalId(recordStudentId);
    if(studentFocusContext?.studentId)return studentForSignalId(studentFocusContext.studentId);
    return null;
  }
  if(page==='programs'&&studentFocusContext?.studentId)return studentForSignalId(studentFocusContext.studentId);
  return null;
}

function resetStudentCardHeat(){
  if(!confirm('이 PC에 저장된 학생카드 클릭 색상 이력을 모두 초기화할까요?'))return;
  try{localStorage.removeItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_STORE);}catch{}
  refreshStudentHeatClasses();
  toast('학생카드 색상 이력을 초기화했습니다.');
}
function refreshStudentHeatClasses(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.student-detail-card[data-student]').forEach(card=>{
    const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[])[Number(card.dataset.student)];
    card.classList.remove('heat-0','heat-1','heat-2','heat-3','heat-4','heat-5','heat-6','heat-7');
    card.classList.add(studentHeatClass(student));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-student-heat-period]').forEach(button=>button.classList.toggle('active',Number(button.dataset.studentHeatPeriod)===studentCardHeatPeriodDays()));
}

function refreshDormRulesFromReadonly({rerender=true,silent=true}={}){
  const rules=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormRules)?readonlyCache.dormRules:[];
  if(rerender&&dormMode==='outing')render('dorm');

  return rules.length>0;
}

  if(rerender&&dormMode==='outing')render('dorm');
  if(!silent)toast(rules.length?`학사운영규칙 ${rules.length}건이 연결되어 있습니다.`:'학사운영규칙을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  return rules.length>0;
}

function dormRuleNormalizeDateTime(value){
  if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='') return '';
  // Google Sheets 날짜 직렬값은 시트의 '현지 날짜/시간' 값이다.
  // UTC instant로 해석한 뒤 Asia/Seoul을 다시 적용하면 +9시간이 생기므로,
  // 직렬값의 날짜/시간 성분을 그대로 추출한다.
  if(typeof value==='number'||/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(String(value).trim())){
    const serial=Number(value);
    if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
      const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
      if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){

async function deleteDormRule(ruleId){
  const rules=dormRuleHistory(), rule=rules.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(ruleId));if(!rule)return;
  if(!confirm(`${rule.name} 규칙을 규칙로그 시트에서도 삭제할까요?`))return;
  try{
    const result=await window.schoolBoard?.deleteDormRule?.(ruleId);
    if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'삭제 실패');
    setDormRuleCache((readonlyCache?.dormRules||rules).filter(x=>String(x.id)!==String(ruleId)));render('dorm');toast('학사외출 규칙을 시트에서 삭제했습니다.');
  }catch(error){toast(error?.message||'학사외출 규칙 삭제에 실패했습니다.');}
}

function dormSeoulUniversityRows(student){
  const seoul=/서울대|연세대|고려대|서강대|성균관대|한양대|중앙대|경희대|한국외대|서울시립대|건국대|동국대|홍익대|숙명여대|이화여대|광운대|국민대|숭실대|세종대|서울과기대/;
  const base=admissionBase(student.id), actual=base.actual9;
  const cuts=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).filter(x=>seoul.test(String(x.university||''))&&Number.isFinite(Number(x.cut)));
  const grouped=new Map();
  cuts.forEach(row=>{const key=String(row.university||'').trim();if(!key)return;const diff=actual==null?null:actual-Number(row.cut);const old=grouped.get(key);if(!old||Math.abs(diff??99)<Math.abs(old.diff??99))grouped.set(key,{...row,diff});});
  return [...grouped.values()].sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.diff??99)-Math.abs(b.diff??99)).slice(0,9);
}

function dormExitBadge08204(row){
  const status=String(row?.status||'').trim();
  const departed=/퇴사|종료/.test(status)||Boolean(row?.endDate&&status!=='재사');
  if(!departed)return {departed:false,label:''};
  const raw=String(row?.endDate||'').trim();

  return {departed:true,label:m?`퇴사 ${Number(m[1])}/${Number(m[2])}`:'퇴사'};
}

function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = readonlyCache?.programs || [];
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;

function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

function inputCenterBatchStudents(){
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(s=>!String(s.status||s.enrollmentStatus||'').includes('전출'));
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='학급') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1') && String(s.className||classNumberOf(s)||'').replace(/반/g,'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchClass||'1'));
  if(scope==='학년전체') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1'));
  if(scope==='개별') return students.filter(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchStudentNo||'').trim());
  return students;
}
function inputCenterBatchExpectedCount(){
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='사용자지정') return Math.max(0,Number(inputCenterConfig.expectedCount||0));
  return inputCenterBatchStudents().length;
}

function selectionOutputRows(){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const errors=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjectErrors||[];
  const subjects=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjects||readonlyCache?.studentSubjects||[];
  return students.map(student=>{
    const studentErrors=errors.filter(error=>
      String(error.studentId||"")===String(student.id||"")
      || String(error.studentNo||"")===String(student.studentNo||"")
    );
    const studentSubjects=subjects.filter(row=>
      String(row.studentId||"")===String(student.id||"")
      || String(row.studentNo||"")===String(student.studentNo||"")
    );
    return {student,errors:studentErrors,subjects:studentSubjects,status:studentErrors.length?"상담 필요":"정상"};
  }).filter(row=>{
    if(outputCenterState.errorFilter==="error")return row.errors.length>0;
    if(outputCenterState.errorFilter==="normal")return row.errors.length===0;
    return true;
  });
}

function findUserAccount(name,email){
  const n=String(name||'').trim(),e=String(email||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const active=activeUserAccounts();
  const byEmail=active.find(a=>String(a.email||'').trim().toLowerCase()===e);
  if(byEmail)return byEmail;
  const exact=active.find(a=>String(a.name||'').trim()===n&&String(a.email||'').trim().toLowerCase()===e);
  if(exact)return exact;
  return bootstrapAdminAccount(n,e);
}

function optionStudents(classNo, selectedId = "") {
  return (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => !classNo || classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id === selectedId ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
}

function scorePeriodKey(row) {
  const year = String(row?.schoolYear || today.getFullYear()).replace(/[^0-9]/g, "") || String(today.getFullYear());
  let semester = String(row?.semester || "").match(/[12]/)?.[0] || "";
  if (!semester && row?.scoreType === "모의고사") {
    const month = Number(String(row.exam || "").match(/(\d{1,2})\s*월/)?.[1] || 0);
    semester = month && month >= 8 ? "2" : "1";
  }
  return `${year}-${semester || "1"}`;
}

function scorePeriodKey(row) {
  const year = String(row?.schoolYear || today.getFullYear()).replace(/[^0-9]/g, "") || String(today.getFullYear());
  let semester = String(row?.semester || "").match(/[12]/)?.[0] || "";
  if (!semester && row?.scoreType === "모의고사") {
    const month = Number(String(row.exam || "").match(/(\d{1,2})\s*월/)?.[1] || 0);
    semester = month && month >= 8 ? "2" : "1";
  }
  return `${year}-${semester || "1"}`;
}

function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {

  if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level

function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
    return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  });
  return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;

function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return "-";
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return escapeHtml(String(value));
  return Number.isInteger(number) ? String(number) : number.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.0+$/, "");
}

  return uepCommonWeightedAverage(rows,row=>Number(row.level));
}

function uepCommonCourseKey(value){
  return String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/[ⅠⅡⅢ]/g,ch=>({'Ⅰ':'I','Ⅱ':'II','Ⅲ':'III'}[ch]||ch)).replace(/[*＊]/g,'');
}
function uepCommonCreditOf(row){
  const key=uepCommonCourseKey(row?.subject||row?.course||row?.courseName||row?.['과목명']);
  const curriculumRows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.curriculumDb)?readonlyCache.curriculumDb:[];
  const credits=[...new Set(curriculumRows.filter(c=>uepCommonCourseKey(c?.['과목명'])===key).map(c=>Number(c?.['이수단위'])).filter(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0))];
  if(credits.length===1)return credits[0];
  const direct=[row?.credit,row?.credits,row?.courseCredit,row?.unitCredit,row?.['이수학점'],row?.['이수단위'],row?.['학점']].map(Number).find(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0);
  if(direct)return direct;
  console.warn('[UEP common weighted grade] credit unresolved',{subject:row?.subject||row?.['과목명'],row});
  return null;
}

}

function uepCommonWeightedAverage(items,valueFn,rowFn=(x=>x)){

function fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment() {
  const internal = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.scoreType === "내신");
  const counts = new Map();
  internal.forEach(row => {
    const value = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""));
    if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) counts.set(value, (counts.get(value) || 0) + 1);
  });
  const frequent = [...counts.entries()].sort((a,b) => b[1]-a[1] || b[0]-a[0])[0]?.[0];
  return frequent || (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(student => !/전출|자퇴|퇴학/.test(String(student.status || "재학"))).length || 259;
}
function fiveGradeBoundaryData(enrollment) {
  const total = Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(enrollment) || 1));
  const cumulativeRates = [0.10, 0.34, 0.66, 0.90, 1];
  const cumulative = cumulativeRates.map(rate => Math.round(total * rate));
  cumulative[cumulative.length - 1] = total;
  const counts = cumulative.map((value, index) => value - (index ? cumulative[index - 1] : 0));
  return { total, cumulative, counts };
}

function actualNineGrade(row) {
  let rank = Number(String(row?.rank ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  let enrollment = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  let derived=false;
  if((!Number.isFinite(rank)||rank<=0)&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(row?.rawScore))){
    const raw=scoreNumeric(row.rawScore);
    const peers=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(peer=>
      peer?.scoreType==="내신"&&String(peer.exam||"")===String(row?.exam||"")&&String(peer.subject||"")===String(row?.subject||"")&&
      String(peer.year||"")===String(row?.year||"")&&String(peer.semester||"")===String(row?.semester||"")&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore))
    );
    if(peers.length){
      rank=peers.filter(peer=>scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore)>raw).length+1;
      if(!Number.isFinite(enrollment)||enrollment<=0)enrollment=peers.length;
      derived=true;
    }
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(rank) || !Number.isFinite(enrollment) || rank <= 0 || enrollment <= 0) return null;
  const percentile = rank / enrollment * 100;
  const grade = percentile <= 4 ? 1 : percentile <= 11 ? 2 : percentile <= 23 ? 3 : percentile <= 40 ? 4 : percentile <= 60 ? 5 : percentile <= 77 ? 6 : percentile <= 89 ? 7 : percentile <= 96 ? 8 : 9;
  return { grade, percentile, rank, enrollment, derived, detail:`${rank}/${enrollment}명 = 상위 ${percentil

===== readonly cache assignment #190 @ 470341 =====
${percentile.toFixed(1)}%${derived?' · 원점수 기준 추정석차':''}` };
}
function actualNineAverage(rows) {
  const details = rows.map(row=>({row,result:actualNineGrade(row)})).filter(item=>item.result);
  const average = details.length ? details.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+item.result.grade,0)/details.length : null;
  return { average, details };
}

function uepRowsForStudent(map,student){
  const id=String(student?.id||student||'').trim(), no=String(student?.studentNo||'').trim();
  const a=id?(map.get(id)||[]):[], b=no?(map.get(no)||[]):[];
  if(!a.length)return b;if(!b.length||a===b)return a;
  return [...new Set([...a,...b])];
}

    const ai=order.indexOf(a),bi=order.indexOf(b); return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
}

    if(direct)return direct;

    return null;

function universityNameKey(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)|\[[^\]]*\]/g,"")
    .replace(/대학교/g,"대")
    .replace(/캠퍼스/g,"")
    .replace(/\s+/g,"")
    .replace(/[()·.\-_/]/g,"");
}
function universityMatches(value, query) {
  if (!query) return true;
  const a=universityNameKey(value), b=universityNameKey(query);
  return a.includes(b) || b.includes(a);
}

function isTrueClubActivity(item){
  const text=`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`;
  return /동아리/.test(text)&&!isVolunteerActivity(item)&&!/\(동아리활동\)/.test(text);
}

function canonicalReportKey(report){
  const pid=unifiedProgramId(reportProgramId(report));
  if(pid)return `id:${pid}`;
  const name=[report?.reportGroup,report?.programName,report?.programTitle,report?.activityTitle,report?.title]
    .map(recordProgramMatchName).find(Boolean)||'';
  return name?`name:${name}`:`report:${String(report?.id||report?.reportId||'')}`;
}
function studentCanonicalReports(student){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[])
    .filter(r=>r&&r.submitted!==false&&studentMatches(r,student)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(r,student))
    .slice().sort((a,b)=>reportTimestampValue(b)-reportTimestampValue(a)||Number(b?.submissionCount||1)-Number(a?.submissionCount||1));
  const map=new Map();
  for(const row of rows){const key=canonicalReportKey(row);if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,row);}
  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>reportTimestampValue(a)-reportTimestampValue(b)||String(a.reportGroup||a.programName||'').localeCompare(String(b.reportGroup||b.programName||''),'ko'));
}

function unifiedProgramSemester(program){
  const explicit=String(program?.semester||program?.term||"").trim();
  if(/1/.test(explicit))return "1학기";
  if(/2/.test(explicit))return "2학기";
  const month=Number(String(program?.date||program?.startDate||"").slice(5,7));
  return month&&month<=7?"1학기":month?"2학기":"";
}

function recordAreaLimit(area){
  const rules=readonlyCache?.recordRuleRows||[];
  for(const row of rules){
    const text=Object.values(row).join(" ");
    if((area==="autonomy"&&/자율/.test(text))||(area==="career"&&/진로/.test(text))||(area==="behavior"&&/행동|종합의견/.test(text))){
      const numbers=[...text.matchAll(/(\d{3,4})\s*(?:자|글자|byte|바이트)/gi)].map(m=>Number(m[1]));
      if(numbers.length)return numbers[0];
    }
  }
  return area==="career"?700:500;
}

function careerDateOverride(program,student=currentRecordStudent()){
  if(!program||!student||recordProgramArea(program)!=="career"||program.kind!=="common")return null;
  const activityId=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-/,"");
  const classNo=recordStudentClass(student);
  const key=recordCareerDateKey(activityId,classNo);
  try{
    const value=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||"null");
    if(!value)return null;
    const range=semesterRangeForProgram(program);
    if(value.date&&(value.date<range.start||value.date>range.end)){
      if(value.confirmed||value.status==="edited"||value.status==="confirmed"){
        return {...value,rangeWarning:true,source:`${value.source||"담임 저장"} · 현재 프로그램 학기범위와 다름`};
      }
      localStorage.removeItem(key);
      return null;
    }
    return value;
  }catch{
    localStorage.removeItem(key);
    return null;
  }
}

function recordRuleField(row,names){
  for(const name of names){
    const key=Object.keys(row||{}).find(k=>String(k).replace(/\s/g,"").includes(name));

  }
  return "";
}

function programWithOverride(program) {
  if (!program || typeof program !== "object") return program;
  const override = state.programOverrides?.[program.id] || {};
  const merged = {...program, ...override, students: program.students || []};
  // 보고서 폼 URL은 프로그램별 로컬 override가 아니라 00_UEP설정의 공통값이 원본입니다.
  // 과거 로컬 빈값/구버전 override가 공통 URL을 가리지 않도록 화면 직전에도 표준 설정값을 우선 적용합니다.
  const uepSettings = readonlyCache?.uepSettings || {};
  if (program.kind === "selected") merged.reportFormUrl = String(uepSettings.SELECTED_ACTIVITY_REPORT_FORM_URL || program.reportFormUrl || "").trim();
  if (program.kind === "common") merged.reportFormUrl = String(uepSettings.COMMON_ACTIVITY_REPORT_FORM_URL || program.reportFormUrl || "").trim();
  return merged;
}
function programAreaKind(program) {
  if (program?.kind === "after" || /방과후/.test(String(program?.type || ""))) return "after";
  const text = `${program?.type || ""} ${program?.recordTitle || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || program?.title || ""}`;
  if (/진로/.test(text)) return "career";

function uniqueActiveAlertStudents(rows=[]){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const validIds=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.id||s.studentId||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
  const validNos=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.studentNo||s.number||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
  const seen=new Set(),out=[];
  for(const row of rows){
    const id=String(row?.studentId||row?.id||'').trim(),no=String(row?.studentNo||row?.number||'').trim();
    const hasKnownIdentity=(id&&validIds.has(id))||(no&&validNos.has(no));
    if((validIds.size||validNos.size)&&!hasKnownIdentity)continue;
    const key=(id&&validIds.has(id)?id:'')||(no&&validNos.has(no)?no:'')||`${row?.classNo||row?.classLabel||''}|${row?.name||''}`;
    if(!key||seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);out.push(row);
  }
  return out;
}

function dashboardTimetableAutoMode(){
  if(dashboardTimetableMode)return dashboardTimetableMode;
  const now=new Date(),minute=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();
  const end=(now.getDay()===3)?(14*60+30):(16*60+30);
  return minute>=end?"night":"day";
}

function dashboardProgramNightSlot(program){

  if(/야간심화/.test(text)){

  }
  return "오후자습";
}

function resolveDashboardStudent(row){
  const students=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.students)?readonlyCache.students:[];
  const ids=[row?.studentId,row?.studentID,row?.id,row?.studentNo,row?.studentNumber].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const name=String(row?.name||row?.studentName||row?.displayName||'').trim();
  const gc=recordGradeClass(row);
  return students.find(student=>{
    const studentIds=[student?.id,student?.studentId,student?.studentID,student?.studentNo,student?.studentNumber].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if(ids.some(id=>studentIds.includes(id)))return true;
    if(name&&name===String(student?.name||student?.studentName||student?.displayName||'').trim()){
      const studentGc=recordGradeClass(student);
      return (!gc.grade||!studentGc.grade||gc.grade===studentGc.grade)&&(!gc.classNo||!studentGc.classNo||String(Number(gc.classNo))===String(Number(studentGc.classNo)));
    }
    return false;
  })||null;
}

function dashboardCounselCandidates(){
  const signature=dashboardCounselCacheSignature(), cached=__uepDashboardCounselCache;
  if(cached&&signature.every((v,i)=>cached.signature[i]===v))return cached.rows;
  const all=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).map(counselPriorityCandidateForStudent).filter(Boolean);
  const active=all.filter(candidate=>{
    const alertId=`${candidate.group||'growth'}:${candidate.stage||'manage'}:${candidate.student.id}`;
    return !counselCandidateCompleted(candidate)&&!autoNoticeIsDismissed('counsel',alertId);
  });
  const byClass=new Map();
  active.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo),'ko')).forEach(candidate=>{
    const cls=String(classNumberOf(candidate.student)||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
    if(!cls||Number(cls)<1||Number(cls)>9)return;
    if(!byClass.has(cls))byClass.set(cls,candidate);
  });
  const rows=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>byClass.get(String(i+1))).filter(Boolean);
  __uepDashboardCounselCache={signature,rows,all,byClass};
  return rows;
}

function growthCounselRecords(studentId){
  const connected=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.counselRecords)?readonlyCache.counselRecords:[];
  const local=Array.isArray(state.growthCounselRecords)?state.growthCounselRecords:[];
  const merged=[...connected,...local.filter(x=>!connected.some(y=>String(y.id)===String(x.id)))];
  const teacher=String(currentLoginTeacherName()||'').trim();
  if(!teacher)return [];
  return merged.filter(row=>String(row.studentId)===String(studentId)&&String(row.writer||'').trim()===teacher).sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||'')));
}

function signalRowsForStudent(student){
  const id=String(student?.id||student?.studentNo||'');
  const no=String(student?.studentNo||'');
  const match=r=>String(r?.studentId||r?.학생ID||'')===id||String(r?.studentNo||r?.학번||'')===no;
  return {
    aid:(readonlyCache?.educationSupport||readonlyCache?.educationAid||[]).filter(match),
    care:(readonlyCache?.protectedStudents||readonlyCache?.healthCare||[]).filter(match),
    risk:(readonlyCache?.riskStudents||readonlyCache?.crisisStudents||[]).filter(match),
  };
}

}

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}

function programStableKey(program){
  const id=String(program?.programId||program?.activityId||program?.id||"").trim();
  if(id)return `${id}|${program?.date||""}`;
  return `${program?.recordTitle||program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}|${program?.date||""}|${program?.time||""}`;
}
function reportProgramId(report){
  return String(report?.programId||report?.activityId||report?.sourceProgramId||report?.linkedProgramId||"").trim();
}

function studentLatestInternalRows(student){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>row.studentId===student.id&&row.scoreType==='내신'&&Number.isFinite(Number(row.level))&&!/산출제외/.test(String(row.note||row.status||'')));
  if(!rows.length)return [];
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))];
  const exam=exams[exams.length-1]||'';
  return exam?rows.filter(row=>row.exam===exam):rows;
}
function studentAllOneGrade(student){
  const rows=studentLatestInternalRows(student);
  return rows.length>0&&rows.every(row=>Number(row.level)===1);
}

function studentDormBadge(student){
  const same=row=>String(row?.studentId||row?.id||'')===String(student?.id||'')||String(row?.studentNo||'')===String(student?.studentNo||'');
  const current=(readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[]).filter(same);
  const past=(readonlyCache?.pastDormStudents||[]).filter(same);
  const has1=past.some(row=>/1/.test(String(row.semester||row.term||'')))||past.length>0;
  const has2=current.some(row=>/2/.test(String(row.semester||row.term||'')))||current.length>0;
  if(has1&&has2)return {label:'학사 · 연간',rows:[...past,...current]};
  if(has2)return {label:'학사 · 2학기',rows:current};
  if(has1)return {label:'학사 · 1학기',rows:past};
  return null;
}

  return unique;
}

===== readonly cache assignment #345 @ 919650 =====
AdmissionCutLimit(value){
  if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return null;
  const direct=Number(value);if(Number.isFinite(direct)&&direct>=1&&direct<=9)return direct;
  const values=(String(value).replace(/,/g,'').match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g)||[]).map(Number).filter(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=1&&v<=9);
  if(!values.length)return null;
  // 복수 숫자가 들어간 문자열은 더 엄격한 값(작은 등급)으로 판정하여 오탐을 막습니다.
  return Math.min(...values);
}

function sendProgramEmailNotice(program){
  const recipients=[...new Set(programStudentMasters(program).map(s=>String(s.email||"").trim()).filter(v=>v&&v!=="-"&&/@/.test(v)))];
  if(!recipients.length)return toast("학생 이메일이 연결된 출석부 인원가 없습니다.");
  const subject=encodeURIComponent(`[UEP] ${program.actualTitle||program.title||"프로그램"} 안내`);
  const body=encodeURIComponent(programNoticeText(program));
  window.open(`mailto:?bcc=${encodeURIComponent(recipients.join(","))}&subject=${subject}&body=${body}`,"_self");
  toast(`${recipients.length}명의 메일 발송 화면을 열었습니다.`);
}

function allConnectedReports(){
  const reports=[];
  (readonlyCache?.students||[]).forEach(student=>(student.reports||[]).forEach(report=>reports.push({...report,__student:student})));
  (readonlyCache?.programs||[]).forEach(program=>(program.reportReports||[]).forEach(report=>reports.push({...report,__program:program})));
  const unique=new Map();
  reports.forEach(report=>{if(report?.id&&!unique.has(String(report.id)))unique.set(String(report.id),report);});
  return [...unique.values()];
}

async function refreshReadonlyAfterNotice({force=false}={}){
  // 확인/승인 상태는 먼저 로컬 캐시에 즉시 반영한다. 매 클릭마다 전체 시트를 다시 읽지 않는다.
  if(force){
    const sync=await window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync?.();
    if(sync?.ok&&sync.data){readonlyCache=sync.data;return;}
  }
  const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();
  if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data;
}

function updateDormOutingCount() {
  const key = dateKey(today);
  const count = (readonlyCache?.dormOutings||[]).filter((item) => item.date === key && !dormOutingExcludedByRule(item)).length || 0;
  const badge = $("#dormOutingCount");
  if (badge) badge.textContent = count;
}

  $("#taskDate").value = dateKey(today);
  $("#modalBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  setTimeout(() => $("#taskTitle").focus(), 30);
}

  $("#modalBackdrop").classList.add("hidden");
}

function uepExpectedGrade(rank,total){
  if(!total||!rank)return "-";
  const rate=rank/total;
  return rate<=.10?"1":rate<=.34?"2":rate<=.66?"3":rate<=.90?"4":"5";
}

function uepDormStudentIds(){
  return new Set((readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[]).filter(x=>!/퇴사/.test(String(x.status||""))).map(x=>String(x.studentId||x.studentNo||"")));

function uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,term){
  const keys=Object.keys(row).filter(key=>key.startsWith(term+" ")&&/선택\d+|정보·외국어|예술/.test(key));
  return keys.map(key=>String(row[key]||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
}
function uepStudentGradeAverage(studentId){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(x=>String(x.studentId||"")===String(studentId||"")&&x.scoreType==="내신"&&Number.isFinite(Number(x.level)));
  if(!rows.length)return null;
  return rows.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+Number(x.level),0)/rows.length;
}
function uepExpectedGrade(rank,total){
  if(!total||!rank)return "-";
  const rate=rank/total;
  return rate<=.10?"1":rate<=.34?"2":rate<=.66?"3":rate<=.90?"4":"5";
}
function uepSelectionErrors(row){
  const errors=[];
  const terms=["2-1","2-2"];
  const science=/(물리|화학|생명과학|지구과학|역학|에너지|전자기|양자|세포|물질대사|유전|지구시스템|융합과학)/;
  const social=/(세계사|동아시아|사회와 문화|사회문화|윤리|지리|정치|법과 사회|경제|인문학|사회문제)/;
  terms.forEach(term=>{const subjects=uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,term);if(subjects.some(x=>science.test(x))&&subjects.some(x=>social.test(x)))errors.push({type:"문·이과 교차지원",term,detail:subjects.join(" · ")});});
  const first=uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,"2-1"),second=uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,"2-2");
  const hierarchy=[
    [/(역학과 에너지|전자기와 양자)/,/물리학/ ,"물리"]

===== readonly cache assignment #467 @ 1288361 =====
예술"]||""),art2=String(row["2-2 예술"]||"");
  const a1=/음악/.test(art1)?"음악":/미술/.test(art1)?"미술":"";
  const a2=/음악/.test(art2)?"음악":/미술/.test(art2)?"미술":"";
  if((a1||a2)&&a1!==a2)errors.push({type:"예술과목 연계오류",term:"2-1 → 2-2",detail:`${art1||"미신청"} → ${art2||"미신청"}`});
  if(!first.length||!second.length)errors.push({type:"신청 누락",term:!first.length?"2-1":"2-2",detail:"해당 학기 선택과목이 비어 있습니다."});
  return errors;
}

  const byId=new Map(),byNo=new Map();

    const id=String(row?.['학생ID']||'').trim(), no=String(row?.['학번']||'').replace(/\.0$/,'').trim();

  });

      __student:student,

    };

  async function refreshSelection51FromSheet(){
    if(inFlight)return inFlight;
    const now=Date.now();
    if(now-lastStartedAt<MIN_INTERVAL_MS)return false;
    lastStartedAt=now;
    inFlight=(async()=>{
      try{
        // Full readonly sync is intentional here: 51 lives in the data-processing
        // spreadsheet and is not part of the lightweight operational-range sync.
        // This guarantees readonlyCache.selectionErrorRows is rebuilt from current 51.
        const ok=await refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:false});
        if(ok&&isSelectionWorkspace())render('records');
        return !!ok;
      }catch(error){
        console.warn('[UEP] 51 selection-error live refresh failed',error);
        return false;
      }
    })();
    try{return await inFlight;}finally{inFlight=null;}
  }

  const base=window.uepSelectionDataset||uepSelectionDataset;
  if(typeof base==='function'){
    const wrapped=function(){
      if(isSelectionWorkspace())queueMicrotask(()=>refreshSelection51FromSheet());

    };

    try{uepSelectionDataset=wrapped}catch{}

  window.__UEPRefreshSelection51=refreshSelection51FromSheet;
